Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in clusters theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 517 and the invariant I(A,B) = 842 mod 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to clusters structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 517/842 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the clusters space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 358 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 532. QED.
